Little Folk- Music for Babies, Toddlers, & Young Children

Little folk is an early years music class perfectly suited for children aged 4 months - 5 years (or 6 or older) and their parents. Regular class schedule- Monday- Oldcastle Masonic Hall 10am Tuesday- Mullingar Arts Centre 10&11am Wednesday- Kells Family Resource Centre 10&11am Friday- The Backyard, Cavan Town 10&11am Featuring 45 minutes of live music, playing percussive instruments and movement with scarves, a good time is to be had by all. Classes explore rhythm, tempo and movement featuring Kyle’s award winning original children’s music and traditional children's music. Following the Kodaly philosophy of music training, the voice is the first instrument. The sessions are highly interactive and all music is performed live. Kyle has been writing and performing children's music since 2012.

Dublin

Unlock the Power of Play with LEGO-Based Therapy Groups! Is your child on the autism spectrum, or navigating challenges like ADHD, anxiety, or communication difficulties? Imagine a space where they can thrive, building essential social and communication skills through the magic of LEGO. Our LEGO-Based Therapy Groups, designed specifically for neurodiverse children, provide a supportive, fun-filled environment where your child can: ✔️ Learn teamwork and turn-taking ✔️ Enhance listening and communication skills ✔️ Develop confidence while making friends ✔️ Practice real-world problem-solving Facilitated by highly qualified specialists—a Psychologist/Play Therapist and a CBT Psychotherapist—these sessions are tailored to help children aged 6–12 years build confidence, improve social skills, and have a blast while doing so! Quiet time - Coolock Library is introducing quiet time on Monday nights from 6pm to 8pm in our children's library. During that time, we will create a calm and quiet environment in our children's library. Sensory toys will be available, as will ear defenders, if needed. Our sensory Cubbie will be available to use. Lighting will be dimmed, phones will be turned off, people will be encourage to keep their voices down and take mobile calls outside. We will be prioritising customers with extra sensory needs.

Fingal Additional Needs

Dublin

Fingal Additional Needs is a fun and inclusive experience for children with additional needs! Spaces are limited, so book your spot today! Location: The Band Room, Skerries My name is David, and I started Fingal Additional Needs after leaving my previous job. Many parents asked me to continue supporting their loved ones with 1-1 outings, and as a parent of a child with ASD and ADHD, I know how valuable a break can be. For over 10 years, I've worked with children and adults with additional needs. After a successful year offering 1-1 support and two years running Football4All with Skerries Town, I introduced summer camps that were a huge success, leading to extra classes. I've recently set up an afterschool class. My goal is to give parents and carers a well-deserved break, while providing a fun and engaging environment for children. I'm not a therapist, but I focus on creating meaningful and enjoyable experiences for the kids. Whether it's getting active or socializing or just happy to be in there own space these sessions are designed with your child's needs in mind, whether they have ADHD, autism, Down syndrome, or other additional needs. You can check out some of the work I’ve done on Instagram and Facebook @fingaladditionalneeds.

View full details →

Finglas Family Resource Centre- Toddler Group and More

Dublin

Finglas West Family Resource Centre is creating a welcoming and inclusive space where people have a sense of belonging. They have a great selection of classes and groups for families. Reach out to them by phone or email to get more information on attending the groups/times. 📍Location: Finglas West Family Resource Centre, Barnardos, Off Barry Avenue, Finglas West, Dublin 11, D11HNF4 🧸Contact Details: you can contact finglaswestfrc@barnardos.ie for any queries Peeps Parent & Toddler Group Parents and toddlers will learn and have fun through creative and sensory play activities, stories and songs. Peeps aims to help children become confident learners through play. The programme focuses on five strands of child development: personal, social and emotional development, communication and language, early literacy, early maths and health and physical development. You must register to attend. Fathers Toddler Group Each themed session provides stories, songs and an activity to promote social skills, sensory play and the bond between children and caregiver. Baby Massage This is a lovely space for parents/guardians and babies to meet others, share experiences and learn new techniques to relax and soothe your baby. Toddler Groups with additional Parenting Support in Dublin

Dublin

Shanganagh, Loughlinstown, Ballyogan, Mounttown, Balally and Hillview have Parent and Toddler Groups provided by Southside Partnership. (contact the group to be referred in to the PEEP Programme) A friendly, weekly parent and baby/toddler groups. We use a lovely programme called PEEP, Parents Early Education Learning Together Programme, which helps us all understand about baby and toddler development, and what it is like to become a new parent. Your baby/toddler learns to play with you, their most important person, while also learning how to play alongside other very young children. We have free play, snack time, circle time, song and storytime, and you get to talk to other parents and the staff. The PEEP+ groups have been designed with you in mind! Adventure Avenue Ltd

Meath

Adventure Avenue Ltd is a vibrant, purpose-built children’s role play and learning centre located in Ashbourne, Co. Meath, Ireland. Designed as a colourful mini town, Adventure Avenue invites children aged 0–10 years to explore imagination, creativity, and learning through hands-on role play. Babies under 7 months old attend free of charge. Our interactive streets feature child-sized buildings such as a supermarket, salon, emergency services, and other familiar community spaces, encouraging social skills, confidence, communication, and learning through play. Adventure Avenue offers inclusive 1.5-hour play sessions with parent or guardian supervision required at all times. Our friendly Adventure Avenue team is always on hand to help, ensuring a safe, supportive, and enjoyable experience for every child. We welcome open play, group visits, special occasions, school tours, preschools, crèches, and group bookings. Visits are aligned with Aistear and tailored to suit the age and needs of each group, providing a fun, safe, and engaging learning environment.

Meath

My Mum & Baby Pilates class is a gentle and supportive postnatal exercise class designed to help mothers rebuild strength, improve posture, and reconnect with their bodies after birth — all while keeping baby close by. Classes focus on pelvic floor recovery, core strengthening, mobility and body alignment in a welcoming, baby-friendly environment. Stick around for a cup of tea and chat afterwards and get to know other mums at a similar stage. No previous Pilates experience is needed. For further information or to register, please contact culabulayouth@gmail.com **The Polish Children’s club is a volunteer led initiative open to all children in the community. The club aims to share the gift of Polish language and culture through fun activities, crafts, games, books and family events. **CoderDojo is a global movement of free, open, volunteer-led coding clubs (Dojos) where young people aged 7–17 (Ninjas) can explore digital technology with the support of their fellow Ninjas and volunteer mentors. CoderDojo’s mission is to give young people around the world the opportunity to learn to code in a social and safe environment. trim_frc.ie@coderdojo.com ** FREE PIANO classes are available to children aged 6 years and above. Lessons are taught by a qualified tutor. To book your child’s place contact us here at the FRC. Thanks to MAEDF grant from Louth and Meath ETB we are able to loan all equipment needed so children can access online classes from home. Nominal fee applies to loan of equipment to cover insurance and maintenance costs.

Rathanna Parent, Baby and Toddler Group in Carlow is a weekly playgroup for parents/ caregivers, grandparents/childminders and their little ones from newborn to preschool. What to expect ?? You may arrive & leave anytime during playgroup session and typically everyone helps to tidy up for the last 5-10mins of the session & there are changing facilities available. We are located in one big hall that are filled with toys appropriate for 0-5 years and include small ride on cars/ tractors, prams, Lego, trains, ball pit, reading corner and soft play area on large soft mats to protect the little ones. The group also has a parents walking group which operates at certain times throughout the year. Walk the gorgeous Rathanna Sli na Slainte route (5.4km) - check out their socials to see if the group is operating. (weather and season depending) 📍Location: Rathanna Community Hall Rathanna, Ireland R95HH68 🗓️⏰Time: Every Thursday from 9:30am -11:30am (during school term) 💶 Cost: €3 per Family 🧸Contact Details: you can contact 0857074738 for any queries

Cavan

Adcentre Kilmore Parent, Toddler and Baby Group in Cavan is moderated by Susuana with the support of the Local African Caribbean Migrant communities living in the town. The forum provide a platform to support the ethnic minority of African Caribbean communities in parenting with much respect and regards for our own cultural believe. This is the very first of is kind here in Ireland initiated by Susuana that focus on the African Caribbean cultural background of parenting and support for the community. Art Room 109

Galway

Play and Create by Art Room 109 Classes are designed for parents and walking toddlers aged 1 to 5. These 60-minute sessions are flexible to fit your little one’s routine—arrive and leave at a time that suits you. The final 10 minutes feature bubbles, music, and parachute play. Each session is thoughtfully crafted to engage both parents and toddlers in a fun and dynamic way. With up to six different open-ended art stations, this is a completely child-led class focused on play-based learning and the development of fine motor, social, and creative skills. Parents must accompany and supervise their child throughout, as classes are fully child-led with no formal teaching—my role is to guide and facilitate creativity. All sensory materials are freshly homemade, natural, and taste-safe. Washable, non-toxic paints are used, and we recommend dressing children in pre-loved clothes. These laid-back sessions encourage connection with other parents and the community while inspiring simple creative play you can try at home. Based in Kinvara, Co. Galway, Art Room 109 travels up to one hour from H91 N5P2 to bring creative workshops to schools, community centres, and private venues. Current Class Locations: Maree, Oranmore: Tuesdays and one Sunday a month Kinvara: Fridays glór, Ennis: Thursdays (once a month from 2025) To view the class timetable and book a class, visit buk.ie/109. Follow our creative journey on Instagram: @art_room_109.

Maia Purposeful Play

Clare

Discover a world of child-led play, learning and meaningful engagement for your little ones at Maia Purposeful Play. Our mission is to nurture curious minds and foster holistic development through child-led, interactive and carefully planned play experiences. From sensory play to imaginative play and creative exploration, we offer a range of thoughtfully curated experiences that spark imagination, encourage critical thinking, and promote social skills. Our carefully designed activities are aimed at enhancing children's cognitive, emotional, and physical development while making every moment a memorable adventure. Maia Purposeful Play classes are child-led, meaning children choose their play path and can move freely between the play areas and resources. This empowering approach not only fosters independence but also allows children to unearth their passions, cultivate curiosity, and create their own meaningful play experiences. During each 60-minute session, parents/caregivers and children are invited to embark on this play journey together.

Stories & Stations

Clare

Stories & Stations is run by primary teacher and toddler mum, Maebh. It begins with child-led play at the fun, educational play stations to aid your toddlers' development and feed their curiosity, their senses and their imagination. Stories and songs are led by Maebh at the Story Station for the last 20 minutes of the class. The class ends with some bubble fun. This class is on at 10am for an hour twice a month generally on a Friday (updates on class dates are on storiesandstations Instagram page). Classes are held at Quin Community Centre, Co Clare in The Abbey Room. The class is for 1-3 year olds but we have had some babies of 6 months - 12 months join us from time to time. It is recommended you contact Maebh if hoping to book a child in under the age of 12 months and she will ensure there are appropriate play stations for your little one. Caregivers are expected to stay for the duration of the class and are responsible for their child / children to ensure their safety. Dress your child in clothes that can get dirty as there will be sensory play stations which may include paint, water, sand and playdough. It is recommended you bring a change of clothes for your child to have the option of clothes get wet or dirty.✨ 💶 Cost: From €10 📣 Contact Details: you can contact storiesandstations@gmail.com for any queries 📍 Location: Quin Community Centre, Quin, Ireland V95 RX75 ⏰ Day/Time: Twice a month (keep an eye on dates on our Social Media) 📍 Location: Break Through Dance Company, Quin Road Business Park, 9, Quin Rd, Ennis, Co. Clare ⏰ Day/Time: Once a month (keep an eye on dates on our Social Media)

Cork Multiples Club - Wilton/Bishopstown - Toddler Group

Cork

Cork Multiples Club is a group where parents can get peer support from other parents of multiples (eg. Twins / Triplets. and more!) The Irish Multiple Births Association (IMBA) was founded in 1996 by parents of multiples. IMBA is a registered charity and is managed by volunteers who are all themselves parents of multiples. As parents of multiples, our volunteers have a unique insight into the issues facing expectant parents and families of multiples during pregnancy, birth and the different stages of childhood. The group also hosts a quarterly information evening for expectant parents. Cork

Mindful Sensory Play is a child-led session for parent and toddler to connect, meet new people, build developmental skills and foster their sense of curiosity and wonder! These sessions are designed to promote the early development of mindfulness concepts through present moment awareness and awareness of the five senses, and important life skill with research shows has lifelong benefits! Babywearing ireland

Galway

Baby Wearing Ireland Sling Meets are peer-to-peer support meetings, facilitated by volunteers who are baby wearers. Peer-to-peer allows those who attend to help each other, share their experiences. Sling meets are informal gatherings so it is not possible for BWI volunteers to give any professional advice. Our sling meets offer a sling library service too, so If you like a particular sling you can rent it for a small fee, take it home and try it for a month. Our sling categories are; stretchy wrap, woven wrap, ring sling, mei tai, and soft structured carriers (ssc) In addition to our in person meets we have a national library ,which slings can be rented from and delivered via courier Meet ups are usually monthly, keep an eye on social media to find out the date of next meet up.

Phonics with Robot Reg Galway

Galway

Join us in Oranmore and Mountbellew in County Galway! Phonics with Robot Reg Galway offers exciting, high-energy classes that introduce children aged 3 months to 5 years to early literacy skills which are the foundations for future reading success! Our award winning classes promote language development, motor skills, and phonological awareness. All classes are developed by a primary school teacher in collaboration with a speech therapist. Classes also delivered by an experience primary school teacher. Baby classes (3-12 months) focus on sensory exploration through nursery rhymes, while Minis (1-2.5 years) and Preschooler (2.5-5 years) classes build foundational skills in language and early literacy skills that are fun and engaging! Our classes run in Mountbellew ( Mondays) and Oranmore ( Tuesdays AND Saturdays) Both parents/carers are welcome to attend with their little one. Join us in a class soon by visiting our website to book your spot! www.phonicswithrobotreg.ie/galway or message Karen on 085 1850 286

Sugradh Creative/Messy Play Toddler Playgroup in Galway

Galway

At Sugradh Creative Classes, each week the theme of our class centres on a much-loved children’s book. The Sensory Trays are specially curated for each child. The sensory tray content designed for the 18–36- month age range allows children a more beneficial experience of sensory activities at their own pace. During the 1-hour class, 10-15 minutes are dedicated to a special time for sharing toys. This facilitates the children engaging in social experiments within a safe environment, contributing to the development of their social skills. As a mother, it is important to me when designing the tray content that all products used in the sensory tray are made from non-toxic materials. Tuesdays - 10:30-11:30 am ( 1 hour ) Athenry - Kardio kids ( Raheen Woods Hotel) for the 18–36-month age range Tuam & Loughrea - Fridays

The Extra Special Parent & Toddler Group

Leitrim

The Extra Special Parent & Toddler Group is an inclusive and welcoming community for children with Down syndrome and their families. Our group is built on equality, friendship, support and fun, creating a safe space where every child and family is valued and celebrated. With over 40 families involved, our group provides opportunities for parents and children to connect, share experiences, build friendships and support one another through every stage of their journey. We organise a wide range of events and activities throughout the year that bring enormous benefits to our members, encouraging inclusion, confidence, wellbeing and community connection. Above all, The Extra Special Parent & Toddler Group is a place of belonging , where families come together, children can thrive, and lasting memories are made.

Ardfert Parent and Toddler Group - Ardfert Community Centre -

Kerry

Join the Parent and Toddler Group in Ardfert Community centre on Wednesday's from 9:00am-11am where children adore the company and space to run around in a safe, secure environment. A small donation of 2 euro is given by all our parents each visit and it goes towards the funding for trips and outings such as pirate petes and swimming in Banna beach hotel, Crazy Caves in Castleisland and Kennedys pet farm. Of course we don’t forget the important things in life and have a supply of tea and coffee to hand at all times. It’s great to meet with people who are in the midst of the same problems, anything from weaning to potty training and more. Regular visits from the local public health nurse and visits from a physiotherapist and a life coach/parenting expert. Goals To help children to develop social skills at their own pace. Building a child's emotional confidence. Supporting children's imagination and creativity. Learning through role-play.

Woodkids Forest School

Kilkenny

Forest school promotes nature connection and wellbeing for adults and children alike. Participants get to play games, explore the forest, make nature crafts, develop their balance on ropes, relax in the hammock, listen to stories and many other activities. Sessions are child-led and facilitated by a professional Forest School Leader. Groups run on Fridays and Saturdays from 10am-11:30 in Jenkinstown, Kilkenny. They are designed for children aged 1.5-years and their accompanying caregiver. Younger siblings (non-walking ) are welcome to attend for free. Each term is 4 -6 weeks . Participants are required to wear waterproofs, warm layer, long sleeves and trousers. Dogs aren't allowed at the session. Toilet and handwashing facilities are available at the park entrance. The path is accessible to buggies and wheelchairs.

Baby Sensory Class - Midwife For You

Laois

A calm and nurturing 4-week Gentle Sensory Class designed to gently stimulate your baby’s developing senses through lights, textures, sounds and movement in a relaxed environment. This class is thoughtfully designed by a registered midwife to provide gentle sensory experiences that support baby’s development while keeping stimulation calm and baby-led. It works beautifully as a follow-on from an Infant Massage Course, but can also be enjoyed as a standalone class for parents and babies. Sessions are relaxed and baby-led, with plenty of time for feeding, changing and settling babies as needed, as well as the opportunity to connect with other parents. Time & Location Classes take place in my private studio in Ballyhyland, Co. Laois. Who can attend Suitable for babies from 3 months to pre crawling. Mums, dads or caregivers such as grandparents are very welcome to attend. Do caregivers stay? Yes, caregivers stay for the duration of the class and take part with their baby. What to bring / wear Babies should wear comfortable clothing. No special equipment is required. Classes are facilitated by a registered midwife, creating a supportive and gentle space for both baby and caregiver.

Rathdowney Children With Additional Needs Social Group

Laois

Incredabilities is a social group dedicated to children with additional needs in Rathdowney and surrounding areas. Parents of these incredible children have come together to advocate on their behalf to ensure that these often forgotten children get every opportunity at inclusion and education in their local area. It is a great place to meet other parents going through the same challenges and a great place to see the kids make friends. The group is for kids aged 3 years to teens. The group is open to everyone as they pride themselves on inclusivity! Guidelines 1. Only parents/guardians, the child and their siblings can be facilitated at our Incredabilities play dates on Saturdays, friends or other non-family members cannot be facilitated 2. Each parent must sign in for themselves and all their family members as they arrive. 3. Parents are responsible for the safety and care of their own children, including siblings. 📍Location: Rathdowney's Youth Club, Clochar Centre, Old Convent Rathdowney, Co Laois 🗓️⏰Time: Every Saturday from 3.30am-5.00pm 💶 Cost: €2 per family 🧸Contact Details: you can contact Chloemartin317@gmail.com for any queries

Bloom Baby Classes North West Ireland

Leitrim

Bloom Baby Classes offer gentle, research-based baby development sessions designed to support bonding, early learning, and sensory exploration. Each week introduces new themes, songs, activities, and opportunities for connection with other parents and caregivers. Who Can Attend? Any primary caregiver is welcome — mum, dad, grandparents, guardians, or trusted caregivers. Siblings can attend if the class allows, please send me a message to confirm. Are Caregivers Required to Stay for the Duration? Yes. Caregivers must stay for the full duration of the class to support their baby, join in with activities, and ensure safety. Do Babies Need to Bring or Wear Anything Special? Comfortable clothing suitable for movement and sensory play A blanket or small mat for baby to lie on (optional) For Caterpillar Club: massage oil can be provided, or caregivers may bring their preferred baby-safe oil. 1. Caterpillar Club (6 weeks–6 months) A calm, nurturing class for younger babies focusing on early bonding, baby massage, gentle sensory play, and age-appropriate developmental activities. Perfect for pre-sitters. 2. Busy Bees (6-15 months) A more active, engaging class for babies who are sitting, moving, or exploring. Includes music, sensory play, early movement activities, and opportunities to practice emerging developmental skills in a safe environment. Location: Breffni FRC - Carrick-on-Shannon Times: Weekly sessions Tuesdays Busy Bees 10am-10.45am Caterpillar Club 11-11.45am & Busy Bees

Bloom Baby Classes North West Ireland

Mayo

Bloom Baby Classes Ballina offer gentle, research-based baby development sessions designed to support bonding, early learning, and sensory exploration. Each week introduces new themes, songs, activities, and opportunities for connection with other parents and caregivers. Who Can Attend? Any primary caregiver is welcome — mum, dad, grandparents, guardians, or trusted caregivers. Siblings can attend if the class allows, please send me a message to confirm. Are Caregivers Required to Stay for the Duration? Yes. Caregivers must stay for the full duration of the class to support their baby, join in with activities, and ensure safety. Do Babies Need to Bring or Wear Anything Special? Comfortable clothing suitable for movement and sensory play A blanket or small mat for baby to lie on (optional) For Caterpillar Club: massage oil can be provided, or caregivers may bring their preferred baby-safe oil. 1. Caterpillar Club (6 weeks–6 months) A calm, nurturing class for younger babies focusing on early bonding, baby massage, gentle sensory play, and age-appropriate developmental activities. Perfect for pre-sitters. 2. Busy Bees (6-15 months) A more active, engaging class for babies who are sitting, moving, or exploring. Includes music, sensory play, early movement activities, and opportunities to practice emerging developmental skills in a safe environment. Location: Ballina Sean Duffy Community Centre Times: Weekly sessions Mondays Busy Bees 11-11.45am & Caterpillar Club 12.15-1pm

Ardtreo Gymnastics Academy

Monaghan

Our Soft Play & GymTots group is a relaxed, play-based session designed for babies, toddlers, and preschoolers to explore movement in a safe, welcoming environment. Children can enjoy soft mats, tunnels, balance beams, obstacle courses, and music while developing coordination, confidence, and social skills. This is a completely child-led, open-play class-perfect for busy little explorers and a lovely opportunity for caregivers to connect, chat, and enjoy time with their little ones. The session is structured enough to feel supported, yet flexible to allow each child to play at their own pace. All equipment is provided. Kids should have soft play-friendly clothing (socks,leggings, t-shirts, tracksuits) and a drink of water. This group welcomes all caregivers, including: Mums / Dads / Grandparents / Guardians / Childminders. Siblings within the 0-5 age range are also welcome. Note: A caregiver must be on-site for the full duration of the session. This ensures safety, comfort, and shared fun between you and your little one, there will be fully qualified coaches on hand to assist. Time & Location Day: Fridays& Sundays Time: Afternoon Venue: Kilmore Community Hub, Monaghan H18HY65 Easy parking and pram-friendly access available. No previous gymnastics experience needed - this group is all about play, movement, and fun!

Bloom Baby Classes Midlands Ireland

Westmeath

Locations: Athlone, Edgeworthstown, Longford, Mullingar, Tullamore. We have two classes on offer; caterpillar club for babies aged 0-6 months and busy bees for babies aged 6-15 months. 🐛 Caterpillar Club (Birth - 6 months) 🐛 A gentle and calm introduction to our musical sensory classes. Activities designed to aid all areas of baby development, with a strong focus on parent and baby bonding. Our Caterpillar club class awakens baby's first senses and stimulates in a fun, yet soothing way, using music, sensory props and lights. We end the class with our amazing chill parachute and a short guided baby massage to encourage a lovely long nap! 🐝 Busy Bees (6 months to 15 months)🐝 A fun, lively musical baby development class that focuses on growing baby's confidence for the big world around them. Encouraging fine and gross motor skills, body awareness, early language skills & rhythm. Building on the gentle activities of Caterpillar Club, this session uses instruments and our amazing branded props to bring on their development in all areas. Our classes have a different theme every week for a whole year. During our theme time, we have fun props, dress up and music related to that weeks theme. Theme time is also a great opportunity for busy bees to interact with each other while exploring our props, heuristic treasure baskets and sensory lights. Our caterpillar babies can work on their tummy time while enjoying the sensory lights and themed props. This is also a great time for our bloom parents to catch up and get to know each other. Our classes run Tuesday: Mucklagh Community Centre, Tullamore Wednesday: Mullingar Shamrocks GAA Club Thursday: Drum Community Centre, Athlone Friday: St Mary's Community Campus, Edgeworthstown

View full details →

Each week introduces new themes, songs, activities, and opportunities for connection with other parents and caregivers. Who Can Attend? Any primary caregiver is welcome — mum, dad, grandparents, guardians, or trusted caregivers. Siblings can attend if the class allows, please send me a message to confirm. Are Caregivers Required to Stay for the Duration? Yes. Caregivers must stay for the full duration of the class to support their baby, join in with activities, and ensure safety. Do Babies Need to Bring or Wear Anything Special? Comfortable clothing suitable for movement and sensory play A blanket or small mat for baby to lie on (optional) For Caterpillar Club: massage oil can be provided, or caregivers may bring their preferred baby-safe oil. 1. Caterpillar Club (6 weeks–6 months) A calm, nurturing class for younger babies focusing on early bonding, baby massage, gentle sensory play, and age-appropriate developmental activities. Perfect for pre-sitters. 2. Busy Bees (6-15 months) A more active, engaging class for babies who are sitting, moving, or exploring. Includes music, sensory play, early movement activities, and opportunities to practice emerging developmental skills in a safe environment. Maia Purposeful Play

Sligo

Discover a world of child-led play, learning and meaningful engagement for your little ones at Maia Purposeful Play. Our mission is to nurture curious minds and foster holistic development through child-led, interactive and carefully planned play experiences. From sensory play to imaginative play and creative exploration, we offer a range of thoughtfully curated experiences that spark imagination, encourage critical thinking, and promote social skills. Our carefully designed activities are aimed at enhancing children's cognitive, emotional, and physical development while making every moment a memorable adventure. Maia Purposeful Play classes are child-led, meaning children choose their play path and can move freely between the play areas and resources. This empowering approach not only fosters independence but also allows children to unearth their passions, cultivate curiosity, and create their own meaningful play experiences. During each 60-minute session, parents/caregivers and children are invited to embark on this play journey together.

The ImagiNation

Sligo

The ImagiNation is a colourful, screen-free role play mini world designed for toddlers, preschoolers and young children up to age 7 (with tiny tag-alongs welcome). Children explore a mini world of real-life role play including a café, supermarket, vets, salon, emergency services and more. Play is child-led, open-ended and designed to support confidence, communication, social skills and imagination. Sessions run at Lyons Department Store, Sligo, with bookable play sessions during the week and on Saturdays, as well as private birthday parties and school or group bookings. A small baby area is available for younger siblings, and caregivers stay and supervise for the duration of the session. Seating is provided for adults with clear visibility across the play space. Children should wear comfortable clothes and socks. No special equipment is needed – just curiosity and imagination.

Toddle On

Wexford

Welcome to Toddle On, where parents and children can bond in a warm and friendly environment. Toddle On play café is a warm, welcoming space where babies and young children can play, explore and learn while parents and carers can relax and connect. Play space is suitable for 0-10 year olds. We offer themed sensory and messy play classes for children aged 2 months to 6 years, designed to support early development through music, movement, storytelling and hands-on play. Classes are tailored by age group and include popular activities such as foam messy play, creative trays and imaginative role-play, all in a safe, nurturing environment. Our activities are thoughtfully crafted to provide your child with an enriching early learning experience while also fostering new friendships in the community. Come join us for a memorable time! Want to know more about the play space? Our play café consists of your rooms: 🌈 Sensory Play Room A calm, engaging space designed especially for babies and toddlers. This room features soft play, lights, textures and sensory toys that encourage early development, curiosity and gentle exploration in a relaxed environment. 🎭 Imaginative Play Room Perfect for little imaginations, this room allows children to role-play and create their own adventures. From pretend play to dressing up and themed setups, it supports social skills, confidence and creative thinking. A comfortable, welcoming area for parents and carers to relax while keeping an eye on their little ones. Enjoy a coffee, chat with other parents and take a breather while your children play safely nearby. 🎨 Creative & Messy Play Classroom A hands-on space where children are encouraged to explore, create and get messy. This room supports fine motor skills and learning through play — without the mess at home! 🎯Games Room The Games Room offers older children a dedicated space to enjoy a mix of active games and imaginative play. From basketball, mini snooker and football to creative play with Barbie, Paw Patrol, superheroes, puppets and dress-up, this room keeps older siblings happily engaged. Our Classes Toddle On Teenies Nurture your bond with your baby through our Toddle On Teenies program. With baby sensory activities, baby beats, lámh and baby massage, you and your little one will create lasting memories while supporting your baby's development. Toddle On Tinies Bond with your growing baby at Toddle On with our Toddle On Tinies program. Explore the world of baby sensory, lámh, messy play and music, using instruments to engage with your little one and bond over a weekly theme. Create lasting memories with your baby at Toddle On. Toddle On Tots Let your tot unleash their artistic imagination at Toddle On with our Toddle on Tots program. Each week, your child will delve into a new theme while learning a new sound, exploring messy play, role play, stories, numeracy, lámh, music, and crafts. Watch your tot develop and grow while having fun in a welcoming and engaging environment. Based in DB fitness and injuries, Unit 5 Pinewood Business Park (beside Lidl in Clonard). Various times depending on child’s age, please see booking page for times. When you book a play session you can drop in anytime that day within the opening hours. All caregivers welcome, required to stay for duration. All resources provided, refreshments available to purchase. Sibling discounts and memberships available. For baby classes advised to have easily accessible outfit for baby massage. For older classes messy play involved so best not to wear a new outfit. Got a birthday coming up? Have full use of the play space and a bubble disco for the amazing price of €100 and have your party with us. Snack bags available to purchase to use as your party bags.
